class="flex-1 outline-none mt-3 text-foreground animate-in fade-in-50 slide-in-from-bottom-1 duration-200" style="animation-duration:0s"><h3 id="via-claudes-custom-connector" class="first:mt-0 mt-6 md:mt-8 mb-3 scroll-m-20 text-lg md:text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground"><a class="anchor" href="#via-claudes-custom-connector">Via Claude&#x27;s custom connector</a></h3><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Step 1 — Active NexLev account</strong></p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">Make sure your NexLev account is active before proceeding.</p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Step 2 — Open Claude Connectors</strong></p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">Go to <strong>claude.ai → Settings → Connectors → Add custom connector</strong>. You can also open the connectors page directly:</p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="font-medium text-primary dark:text-primary-second underline underline-offset-4 decoration-primary/10 dark:decoration-primary-second/10 hover:decoration-primary dark:hover:decoration-primary-second transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1" href="https://claude.ai/customize/connectors">Connect on Claude →<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" class="size-3.5 opacity-70"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M5.22 14.78a.75.75 0 001.06 0l7.22-7.22v5.69a.75.75 0 001.5 0v-7.5a.75.75 0 00-.75-.75h-7.5a.75.75 0 000 1.5h5.69l-7.22 7.22a.75.75 0 000 1.06z" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a></p><img alt="Claude Settings → Connectors → Add custom connector" loading="lazy" width="650" height="350" md:text-base"><strong>Step 3 — Enter the connector details</strong></p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">Fill in the following information exactly as shown:</p><div class="my-4 rounded-xl border border-border/60 overflow-hidden"><div class="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] text-sm"><div class="col-span-2 grid grid-cols-subgrid border-b border-border/60 bg-grey-lighter/30 px-4 py-2.5"><span class="font-medium text-foreground/60 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide">Field</span><span class="font-medium text-foreground/60 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide">Value</span></div><div class="col-span-2 grid grid-cols-subgrid px-4 py-3 border-b border-border/40"><span class="text-foreground/80 font-medium pr-6 self-center">Name</span><span class="self-center"><code class="rounded-md bg-grey-lighter px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.82em] font-medium text-foreground border border-border/50">NexLev</code></span></div><div class="col-span-2 grid grid-cols-subgrid px-4 py-3 border-b border-border/40"><span class="text-foreground/80 font-medium pr-6 self-center">Server URL</span><span class="self-center"><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"><code class="rounded-md bg-grey-lighter px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.82em] font-medium text-foreground border border-border/50 break-all">https://prod.dashboard.nexlev.io/api/claude-mcp</code><button type="button" aria-label="Copy value" title="Copy" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center size-6 rounded-md text-grey-muted hover:text-foreground hover:bg-grey-lighter border border-border/50 transition-all duration-150 flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-copy size-3" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg></button></span></span></div><div class="col-span-2 grid grid-cols-subgrid px-4 py-3 "><span class="text-foreground/80 font-medium pr-6 self-center">Authentication</span><span class="self-center"><code class="rounded-md bg-grey-lighter px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.82em] font-medium text-foreground border border-border/50">OAuth</code></span></div></div></div><img alt="Enter NexLev connector details in Claude" loading="lazy" width="650" height="350" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="rounded-xl my-4 md:my-8 object-contain bg-grey-lighter/20 border border-border/50" style="color:transparent" srcSet="/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-step-3-connector-details.png&amp;w=750&amp;q=75 1x, /_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-step-3-connector-details.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75 2x" src="/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-step-3-connector-details.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75"/><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Step 4 — Approve the connection</strong></p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">After adding the connector, please click the <strong>Connect</strong> button and Claude will redirect you to sign in with your NexLev account.</p><div class="my-4 md:my-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border p-3 md:p-4 bg-orange-50/50 text-orange-900 border-orange-200 dark:bg-orange-950/50 dark:border-orange-800 dark:text-orange-200 inset-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(255,165,0,0.1)] dark:inset-shadow-orange-700/30"><div class="mt-0.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-triangle-alert size-4 text-orange-600 dark:text-orange-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m21.73 18-8-14a2 2 0 0 0-3.48 0l-8 14A2 2 0 0 0 4 21h16a2 2 0 0 0 1.73-3"></path><path d="M12 9v4"></path><path d="M12 17h.01"></path></svg></div><div class="text-[14px] md:text-[15px] leading-relaxed min-w-0"><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Sign in with the right NexLev account.</strong> If you have a paid NexLev plan
(Lite, Pro, or Premium), make sure you log in with that account — your rate
limits are tied to the account you connect. Signing in with a different
account will apply Free plan limits instead.</p></div></div><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Step 5.A — Set tool permissions</strong></p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">Go to <strong>claude.ai → Settings → Connectors</strong>, find NexLev, and click <strong>Configure</strong>. Then set <strong>Tool permissions</strong> to <strong>Always allow</strong> — this lets Claude use your NexLev tools automatically without asking you every time.</p><img alt="Set NexLev tool permissions to Always allow in Claude" loading="lazy" width="650" height="350" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="rounded-xl my-4 md:my-8 object-contain bg-grey-lighter/20 border border-border/50" style="color:transparent" srcSet="/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-step-5-permissions.png&amp;w=750&amp;q=75 1x, /_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-step-5-permissions.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75 2x" src="/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-step-5-permissions.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75"/><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Step 5.B — Allow write/delete tools</strong></p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">In the same permissions panel, scroll down through the tools list and make sure you also set <strong>Always allow</strong> for any write or delete tools (such as <strong>Swipe File</strong>). These are listed separately and require individual approval.</p><img alt="Allow write and delete tool permissions for NexLev in Claude" loading="lazy" width="650" height="350" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="rounded-xl my-4 md:my-8 object-contain bg-grey-lighter/20 border border-border/50" style="color:transparent" srcSet="/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-swipe-file-allow.png&amp;w=750&amp;q=75 1x, /_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-swipe-file-allow.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75 2x" src="/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fdocumentation%2Fmcp%2Fclaude-swipe-file-allow.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75"/><div class="my-4 md:my-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border p-3 md:p-4 bg-blue-50/50 text-blue-900 border-blue-200 dark:bg-blue-950/50 dark:border-blue-800 dark:text-blue-200 inset-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(43,127,255,0.1)] dark:inset-shadow-blue-700/30"><div class="mt-0.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info size-4 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg></div><div class="text-[14px] md:text-[15px] leading-relaxed min-w-0"><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Free plan limitation.</strong> Attempting to use Write and delete tools — on the
Free plan, it will return a message prompting to upgrade NexLev subscription.</p></div></div><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Step 6 — Verify NexLev is active in chat</strong></p><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">Before starting, confirm NexLev is enabled in your Claude chat window. <h2 id="understanding-nexlev-responses" class="mt-8 md:mt-10 mb-3 scroll-m-20 border-b border-border pb-2 text-xl md:text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground first:mt-0"><a class="anchor" href="#understanding-nexlev-responses">Understanding NexLev Responses</a></h2>
<p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">When you use NexLev through Claude, ChatGPT, or Claude Code CLI, you&#x27;ll receive interactive widgets displaying:</p>
<ul class="my-4 md:my-5 ml-4 md:ml-5 list-disc space-y-1.5 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base marker:text-grey-muted/50 [&amp;_ul]:my-1.5 [&amp;_ul]:list-[circle] [&amp;_ul_ul]:list-[square]">
<li class="pl-1"><strong>Channel data</strong> — Comprehensive metrics, performance indicators, and growth analytics</li>
<li class="pl-1"><strong>Sortable results</strong> — Click column headers to sort by different metrics</li>
<li class="pl-1"><strong>Expandable details</strong> — View in-depth information for each result</li>
<li class="pl-1"><strong>Filtering options</strong> — Refine results by various criteria</li>
<li class="pl-1"><strong>Pagination</strong> — Navigate through large result sets</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-4 md:my-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border p-3 md:p-4 bg-emerald-50/50 text-emerald-900 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:border-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-200 inset-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(0,213,190,0.1)] dark:inset-shadow-emerald-700/30"><div class="mt-0.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-lightbulb size-4 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 14c.2-1 .7-1.7 1.5-2.5 1-.9 1.5-2.2 1.5-3.5A6 6 0 0 0 6 8c0 1 .2 2.2 1.5 3.5.7.7 1.3 1.5 1.5 2.5"></path><path d="M9 18h6"></path><path d="M10 22h4"></path></svg></div><div class="text-[14px] md:text-[15px] leading-relaxed min-w-0"><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base"><strong>Pro Tip:</strong> Results are interactive! You can sort, filter, and expand
channels directly within the chat interface for deeper analysis.</p></div></div>
<h2 id="rate-limits" class="mt-8 md:mt-10 mb-3 scroll-m-20 border-b border-border pb-2 text-xl md:text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground first:mt-0"><a class="anchor" href="#rate-limits">Rate Limits</a></h2>
<p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">All limits are <strong>per user, per tool, and reset every 24 hours</strong>.</p>
<div class="my-4 md:my-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border p-3 md:p-4 bg-grey-lighter/50 text-foreground border-border inset-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] dark:inset-shadow-grey-darker/30"><div class="mt-0.5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-file-text size-4 text-grey-muted shrink-0" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 22a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h8a2.4 2.4 0 0 1 1.704.706l3.588 3.588A2.4 2.4 0 0 1 20 8v12a2 2 0 0 1-2 2z"></path><path d="M14 2v5a1 1 0 0 0 1 1h5"></path><path d="M10 9H8"></path><path d="M16 13H8"></path><path d="M16 17H8"></path></svg></div><div class="text-[14px] md:text-[15px] leading-relaxed min-w-0"><p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">Lite and Premium share identical rate limits across all tools. If you&#x27;re on
the Premium plan, the numbers in the Lite column apply to you.</p></div></div>

<p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">With <strong>usage credits</strong> enabled, extra actions past the included allowance cost $0.15 (lite model) or $0.40 (pro model) per generation and $0.10 / $0.25 per AI edit, capped at 30 generations and 30 edits per rolling hour. Failed jobs are refunded automatically.</p>

<p class="leading-7 [&amp;:not(:first-child)]:mt-4 text-grey-muted text-[15px] md:text-base">With <strong>usage credits</strong> enabled, calls past the daily limit cost $0.02 each for <code class="relative rounded-md bg-grey-lighter px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.85em] font-medium text-foreground border border-border/50 break-words">get_channel_promotions</code> and $0.05 each for <code class="relative rounded-md bg-grey-lighter px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.85em] font-medium text-foreground border border-border/50 break-words">get_channel_promotions_realtime</code>.
